- **prerelease**: 1. Ensure you're on the branch you want to prerelease from (does not need to be main). 2. Look at existing tags to determine the next prerelease tag. If there are already rc tags for the current version (e.g. v1.0.0-rc0), increment the rc number (v1.0.0-rc1). If there are no rc tags yet, ask the user what version to prerelease. 3.
